## [2026-10-25T07:37:44Z] Turn the solver into a bounded tool-calling agent with grounded rules

User Prompt (verbatim, secrets redacted):
Apply the fixes as below -  
1. Make it an agent, visibly in code:
- One bounded tool-calling loop: the model calls typed tools until it can call submit_answer.
  - Example tools for this problem: resolve_identity, search_evidence, read_evidence, get_activity, check_policy, submit_answer.
  - Caps: maximum iterations (for example 8), a per-call timeout, and a fallback when a cap is hit.
- Model-driven routing: the model decides which cases need investigation and which tools to call. Code shouldn't hardcode the stage order for the hard parts.
- Deterministic guardrails the model can't override, enforced inside the tools and gates:
  - the time cutoff;
  - policy mapping;
  - citation validity, so a cited id must have come from a tool result;
  - the output schema.

  This keeps our accuracy, and it's the pattern the top first-edition systems used.
- Optionally, a narrow verifier step that re-checks citations and time rules on INCOMPLETE answers. That's a real multi-stage handoff with a distinct job.
- An ablation proving the model matters (agent on vs off). Ours showed the model changed only 12 rows, which tells a judge the LLM is decorative.
2. Prompt and tool craft:
- Tool descriptions written as documentation: purpose, when to use, when NOT to use, argument meanings, return shape, size limits, error cases. Use strict JSON schemas.
- Prompts with role, constraints, output schema, explicit refusal/abstain conditions ("return UNSURE if no evidence before the cutoff supports a state"), an injection stance, and 2 synthetic examples.
- A verifier/critic prompt that has its own job.

3. Robustness:
- Iteration caps and timeouts visible in code.
- Replace dataset-fitted windows and the latency signature with grounded rules. Where one must remain, put it behind a clearly low-confidence fallback.
- Remove the account_00N convention.
- Make every time rule causal, meaning no evidence from after the cutoff.
- Keep the retries, validation, fallbacks and unknown-row handling we already had.

4. Rigor: install as a package (pyproject.toml entry point, no sys.path edits), and remove every unused config option and process constant.

5. Ship what the judge can read. The checks that prove quality should live in the shipped source, not only in devtools/ or the docs. That includes the contract validator, invariants, the determinism test and the scorer.
